Collected is every revenue line but appropriated fund balance, transfers in from other funds included. Spent is every spending line less non-cash entries (depreciation, losses on disposing of assets, pension expense under GASB 68), less transfers to capital project funds (shown separately above), and less the 2025 Water District grant explained by Resolution 2026-640. Budgets are the original adopted ones; the Board amends them during the year. Funds spending under $200,000 and the Debt Service Fund, which only passes payments through, are left out.
